  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~create_deep_entity.
    types : begin of ty_podata.
              include type    ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc=>ts_pocreate.
    types :   potoresults type table of ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc=>ts_getnfadata with default key,

           ls_data1          type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_EXT=>ty_data1,

           wa_vdd            like line of it_vdd,

           it_HEAD           type table of  znfa_header,
           wa_HEAD           like line of it_HEAD,
           nr_range_nr       type  inri-nrrangenr value '01',
           object            type  inri-object  value 'ZNFA_ID',
           toyear            type  inri-toyear,
           number            type char10,
           lv_level          type char02,
           lv_fail           type char1,
           lv_return         type char10,
           ls_hist           type zGNFA_HISTORY,
           lt_emailto        type table of ad_smtpadr,
           lt_emailcc        type table of ad_smtpadr,
           lv_nfaid          type char10,
           lv_approver       type string,
           lv_recipient      type string,
           lv_po             type char10,
           lv_buyer          type string,
           ls_podata         type ty_podata,
           lineitem          type i,
           it_query          type table of zgnfa_query_tab,
           wa_query          like line of it_query,
           lv_eventNO        type zgnfa_item-eventid,
           gs_nfaatt         type zrfq_attachment,
           ls_nfaatt         type zrfq_attachment,
           lt_nfaatt         type table of zrfq_attachment,
           lv_dokar          type draw-dokar,
           lv_doknr          type draw-doknr,
           lv_dokvr          type dokvr,
           lv_doktl          type draw-doktl value '000',
           dokar             type draw-dokar,
           doknr             type draw-doknr,
           dokvr             type dokvr,
           doktl             type draw-doktl,
           lv_sernr          type numc2,
           lv_create_flag    type flag,
           lv_file_name      type filep,
           lo_facade         type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid        type string,
           lv_logintype      type char1,
           lt_request_header type tihttpnvp,
           ls_request_header like line of lt_request_header,
           qcount            type numc2,
           ind               type i,
           lv_len            type i,
           lv_len1           type i.
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*           lv_amt            type zde_nfaamout.
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
"BOC ali 28.09.2026 - NFA approval log (ZGNFA_APPR_LOG)
    data : ls_applog  type zgnfa_appr_log,
           lv_lvl_txt type string.
    field-symbols : <lv_appr_role> type any.
"EOC ali 28.09.2026

    data : header   type  thead,
           lines    type table of   tline,
           ls_lines like line of lines.
    data: lv_offset type i,
          lv_len5   type i,
          ls_line   type tline,
          lt_lines  type standard table of tline.



    lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
    if lo_facade is bound.
      lt_request_header = lo_facade->get_request_header( ).
    endif.
    read table lt_request_header into ls_request_header with key name = 'loginid'.
    if sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_loginid = ls_request_header-value.
      translate lv_loginid to upper case.
    endif.


    data : e_fy type char4.
    call function 'GM_GET_FISCAL_YEAR'
      exporting
        i_date                     = sy-datum
        i_fyv                      = 'V3'
      importing
        e_fy                       = e_fy
      exceptions
        fiscal_year_does_not_exist = 1
        not_defined_for_date       = 2
        others                     = 3.
    if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    endif.

    toyear = e_fy.

    case iv_entity_set_name.
      when 'NFAHeaderSet'.
*
        try.
            io_data_provider->read_entry_data(
              importing
                es_data = ls_data
            ).
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception. " mgw technical exception
        endtry.
**          call function 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
**          exporting
**            object                  = object
**            nr_range_nr             = nr_range_nr
**            toyear                  = toyear
**          importing
**            number                  = number
***            returncode              = returncode
**          exceptions
**            object_not_found        = 01
**            interval_not_found      = 02
**            number_range_not_intern = 03.
**
**ls_data-nfaid = number.
**
        if ls_data-flag ne 'AWARD'.

          loop at ls_data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp assigning field-symbol(<fs>).
*          wa_head-event_no =   |{ <fs>-eventid alpha = in }|.
            wa_data-nfaid = number.
            wa_data-eventid   = |{ <fs>-eventid alpha = in }|.
            wa_data-rfqid     = <fs>-rfqid.
            wa_data-lineitem  = <fs>-rfqlineitem.
            wa_data-prnumber  = <fs>-prnumber.
            wa_data-pritem    = <fs>-pritem.
            wa_data-matnr     = <fs>-material.
            wa_data-maktx     = <fs>-material_des.
            REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN <fs>-rfq_qty WITH ''.
            wa_data-rfq_qty   = <fs>-rfq_qty.
            wa_data-uom       = <fs>-uom.
*          data : ven type  lifnr.
*          condense <fs>-award_supp.
*          ven = <fs>-award_supp.
*          condense ven.
*          ven = |{ ven alpha = in }|.
*          select single name1 from lfa1 into  @wa_data-suppliername
*            where lifnr = @ven.

            wa_data-awardedsupplier    = <fs>-award_supp.
            wa_data-supplierref    = <fs>-supplierref.
            wa_data-suppliername    = <fs>-suppliername.
            wa_data-vddapp     = <fs>-vdd_appli.
            REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN <fs>-totamtgst WITH ''.
            wa_data-totamt   = <fs>-totamtgst.
            wa_data-currency  = <fs>-currency.
            REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN <fs>-nego_savings WITH ''.
            wa_data-negosave  = <fs>-nego_savings.
*          wa_head-createdby   =  sy-uname.
*          wa_head-createdon  = sy-datum.
*          wa_head-createdat = sy-uzeit.
            wa_data-createdby   =  sy-uname.
            wa_data-createdon  = sy-datum.
            wa_data-createdat = sy-uzeit.
            data : gnfa_itm type zgnfa_item.
            select single * from zgnfa_item
              where eventid = @wa_data-eventid
                     and rfqid = @wa_data-rfqid
              and lineitem = @wa_data-lineitem
              and nfaid is initial
              into @gnfa_itm.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              delete zgnfa_item from gnfa_itm.
              if sy-subrc = 0.
                commit work.
              endif.
            endif.
            append wa_data to it_data.
            clear : wa_data.

          endloop.
**
          if it_data is not initial.
*          modify znfa_header from @wa_head.
            modify zgnfa_item from table @it_data.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              copy_data_to_ref(
                exporting
                  is_data = ls_data
                changing
                  cr_data = er_deep_entity
              ).
            else.
              raise exception type /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
                exporting
                  message = 'ErrorIn saving Data'.
            endif.
          endif.
        else.

          lv_eventNO = |{ ls_DATA-eventid alpha = in }|.
*  select from zrfq_item as a left outer join eban as b on ( b~banfn = a~prnumber and b~bnfpo = a~pritem )
*            left outer join t001k as c on ( c~bwkey =  a~werks )
*            fields
*            a~event_no,
*            a~rfqid,
*            a~rfq_qty,
*            a~currency,
*            a~pritem,
*            a~prnumber,
*            a~lineitem,
*            a~maktx,
*            a~matnr,
*            a~werks,
*            a~purgroup,
*            a~plant_add,
*            a~prdate,
*            a~uom,
*            b~ekgrp,
*            b~preis,
*            b~peinh,
*             b~waers,
*            c~bukrs
*
*            where event_no = @lv_eventid and
*    round   = ( select max( round ) from zrfq_item where event_no = @lv_eventid )
*            into table @data(it_AWARD).
*
*
          select event_no ,rfq_no ,unitamt , taxamt ,totamt , currency , prnumber , prlineitem , lineitem , supplier , supplierref , suppliername
            from zrfq_item_bid_re into table @data(it_bidsub) where event_no =  @lv_eventNO
            and supplierref = @ls_DATA-supplierref.

          """""""""""last round
          select  rfq_no , supplierref from zrfq_HEAD_bid_re
            where  event_no = @lv_eventNO  and bidstatus = 'Quoted' and supplierref = @ls_DATA-supplierref
            into table @data(itrfq) .

          sort itrfq  by rfq_no descending.
          data(level) = lines( itrfq ).
          read table itrfq into data(wrfq) index 1.

          select event_no ,rfq_no ,unitamt , taxamt ,totamt , currency , prnumber , prlineitem , lineitem , supplier , supplierref , suppliername
                      from zrfq_item_bid_re into table @data(it_bidrfq) where event_no =  @lv_eventNO
                      and supplierref = @ls_DATA-supplierref and rfq_no = @wrfq-rfq_no.
          """""""'

          loop at ls_data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp assigning field-symbol(<aw>) where check = 'X'.
*data(lastrfqno) =  <aw>-rfqid+12(2) - 1.
            read table it_bidsub into data(wa_bidsub) with key rfq_no = <aw>-rfqid lineitem = <aw>-rfqlineitem.
            if sy-subrc <> 0.

              read table IT_BIDrfq into data(WA_BIDrfq) with key rfq_no = wrfq-rfq_no lineitem = <aw>-rfqlineitem.
              if sy-subrc = 0.
                <aw>-totamtgst =    WA_BIDrfq-totamt.
                <aw>-suppliername = WA_BIDrfq-suppliername.
                <aw>-award_supp =   WA_BIDrfq-supplier.
                <aw>-supplierref =  WA_BIDrfq-supplierref.
              endif.

            else.
              <aw>-totamtgst = wa_bidsub-totamt.
              <aw>-suppliername = wa_bidsub-suppliername.
              <aw>-award_supp = wa_bidsub-supplier.
              <aw>-supplierref = wa_bidsub-supplierref.
            endif.


          endloop.

*          data : amt type  zrfq_item_bid_re-totamt.
*
*          ls_data-eventid = lv_eventid.
*          ls_data-totalnfaamt = reduce dmbtr( init i type dmbtr for w in it_bidsub
*                                          next i = i + w-totamt ).
*
*          ls_Data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp = value #( for wa_AWARD in it_AWARD
*                                                  ( eventid      = wa_AWARD-event_no
*                                                    rfqid        = wa_AWARD-rfqid
*                                                    rfq_qty      = wa_AWARD-rfq_qty
*                                                    material     = wa_AWARD-matnr
*                                                    material_des = wa_AWARD-maktx
*                                                    pritem       = wa_AWARD-pritem
*                                                    prnumber     = wa_AWARD-prnumber
*                                                    rfqlineitem  = wa_AWARD-lineitem
*                                                    uom          = wa_AWARD-uom
*                                                    currency     = wa_AWARD-waers
*                                                    totamtgst    = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = WA_AWARD-prnumber prlineitem = WA_AWARD-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-totamt optional )
*                                                    award_supp    = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = WA_AWARD-prnumber prlineitem = WA_AWARD-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-supplier optional )
*                                                    suppliername    = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = WA_AWARD-prnumber prlineitem = WA_AWARD-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-suppliername optional )
*                                                    supplierref   = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = WA_AWARD-prnumber prlineitem = WA_AWARD-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-supplierref optional )
*
*                                                  ) ).



          copy_data_to_ref(
            exporting
              is_data = ls_data
            changing
              cr_data = er_deep_entity
          ).


        endif.


      when 'NFADetHeaderSet'.
        try.
            io_data_provider->read_entry_data(
              importing
                es_data = ls_data1
            ).
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception. " mgw technical exception
        endtry.


        if ls_Data1-button = 'Q'.

          select * from  zgnfa_query_tab where nfaid = @ls_data1-nfaid
            into table @data(lt_query).

          select single max( counter )
            from zgnfa_query_tab where nfaid = @ls_data1-nfaid
            into @data(qcounter).
          loop at ls_data1-detheadertoquerysetnp assigning field-symbol(<query>).

            lv_len = strlen( <query>-question ).
            lv_len1 = strlen( <query>-question ).

            if lv_len > 255.
              read table lt_query into data(wa_query1) with key
            nfaid = ls_data1-nfaid question = <query>-question+0(255)
            question1 = <query>-question+255.
              if sy-subrc = 0.
                if lv_len1 > 255.
                  update zgnfa_query_tab
                set answer = @<query>-answer+0(255),
                answer1 = @<query>-answer+255
                where nfaid = @ls_data1-nfaid
                  and question = @wa_query1-question
                  and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
                  and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
                else.
                  update zgnfa_query_tab
                set answer = @<query>-answer
                where nfaid = @ls_data1-nfaid
                  and question = @wa_query1-question
                  and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
                  and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
                endif.
              else.
                qcounter  = qcounter + 1.
                wa_query-nfaid  = ls_data1-nfaid.
                wa_query-question = <query>-question+0(255).
                wa_query-question1 = <query>-question+255.
                wa_query-counter  = qcounter.
                if lv_len1 > 255.
                  wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
                  wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
                else.
                  wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
                endif.
                wa_query-querychk = 'X'.
                if <query>-raisedby is initial.
                  select single name from zrfq_user_master
                  into @data(raisedbyname) where id = @lv_loginid
                  and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
                  wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
                else.
                  wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
                endif.
                wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
                wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
                wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
                wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
                append wa_query to it_query.
                clear : wa_query.
              endif.

                      nfaid = ls_data1-nfaid question = <query>-question.

                  wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
                  wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
                else.
                  wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
                endif.

                  select single name from zrfq_user_master
                  into @raisedbyname where id = @lv_loginid
                  and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
                  wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
                else.
                  wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
                endif.
                wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
                wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
                wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
                wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
                append wa_query to it_query.
                clear : wa_query.
              endif.
            endif.
            clear : lv_len,lv_len1.

          select * from zgnfa_item into  table @itnfa_item where eventid = @ls_data1-eventid.

          select event_no , round ,rfq_no,lineitem,prnumber,prlineitem,quotationid,taxamt,unitamt

            into table @it_bidre.

            wa_data-bukrs  = <fs2>-companycode.
            wa_data-awardedsupplier = wa_data-awardedsupplier.

            wa_data-pritem    = <fs2>-prlineitem.

            wa_data-budgetamt     = <fs2>-budgetamt.
            REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN <fs2>-nfaamount WITH ''.
            wa_data-nfaamt     = <fs2>-nfaamount.

            wa_data-createdon  = sy-datum.
            wa_data-createdat = sy-uzeit.

             rfq_no = rfq_no lineitem = wa_data-lineitem.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              wa_data-baseamt = wa_bidre-unitamt.
              wa_data-taxamt = wa_bidre-taxamt.
              wa_data-taxcode = wa_bidre-taxcode.

            clear : wa_data , wa_bidre.

          endloop.

              loop at return into data(ls_return).
                append value #( nfaid        = ls_return-nfaid
                                prnumber     = ls_return-prnumber
                                pritem       = ls_return-pritem

                                ponumber     = ls_return-ponumber
                                message      = ls_return-message ) to ls_podata-potoresults.
                if lt_emailto is not initial and ls_return-ponumber is not initial.
                  "SEND MAIL with lt_emailcc and lt_emailto
                  lv_nfaid = wa_head-nfa_ID.
                  lv_po = ls_return-ponumber.

                      i_recipient = lv_recipient

                      i_content   = 'MAIL4'

                      return      = lv_return                 " Character Field with Length 10
                  ).
                endif.
              endloop.
              copy_data_to_ref( exporting is_data = ls_podata
                                changing  cr_data = er_deep_entity ).
            endif.
          endif.
        endif.
    endcase.

  endmethod.

* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~get_expanded_entity.


    data : ls_data      type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_ext=>ty_data,
           ls_data1     type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_ext=>ty_data1,
           ls_WDATA     type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_ext=>ty_DGNFA,

           ls_keys1     type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_ext=>ts_nfadetheader,
           ls_wkeys     type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc_ext=>ts_nfavwhead,

           lv_level_new type char10.

    data : lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,

           lv_loginid   type string,
           lv_logintype type char1,
           lv_userrole  type char1,
           lv_bukrs     type bukrs,
           lv_lifnr     type lifnr,
           lv_eventid   type char10,
           lv_amt       type p length 7 decimals 2,
           lv_amt_gst   type p length 7 decimals 2.

    types : begin of ty_min,
              lineitem    type zrfq_item_bid_re-lineitem,
              eventid     type zrfq_item_bid_re-event_no,
              supplierref type zrfq_item_bid_re-supplierref,
              vendorname  type zrfq_item_bid_re-suppliername,
              finalquote  type p decimals 2 length 13,
            end of ty_min.

    types: begin of ty_rank,
             vendorname type string,
             tot        type p decimals 2 length 13,
             ranking    type string,
           end of ty_rank.
    data: lt_rank type table of ty_rank,
          ls_rank type ty_rank.

    data : ekgrp        type eban-ekgrp,
           name         type char20,
           e_fy         type  char4,
           e_last_day   type  sy-datum,
           e_first_day  type  sy-datum,
           i_bid        type table of  zcl_zrfq_generalnfa_pr_mpc=>ts_nfacomsummven,
           l_bid        like line of i_bid,
           lt_tcnp      type table of   zrfq_head_bid_re,
           it_linmin    type table of ty_min,
           year_date    type range of sy-datum,
           i_gjahr      type  t009b-bdatj,
           commdif_l1l2 type p decimals 2 length 15.
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*           lv_nfaamt    type zde_nfaamout.
* NOTE : LV_AMT (line 21) is NOT removed - it is a packed field used later
*        for the final-quote totals and has nothing to do with the band.
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*

    data : id               type  thead-tdid,
           language         type  thead-tdspras,
           name1            type  thead-tdname,
           object           type  thead-tdobject,

           lv_justification type  string.

           ls_lines like line of lines.

**-- Get Parameters
    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).

    case iv_entity_set_name.

      when 'NFAHeaderSet'.
        data : amt type  zrfq_item_bid_re-totamt.

        io_tech_request_context->get_converted_keys(
          importing
            es_key_values = ls_keys                " Entity Key Values - converted
        ).

        if ls_keys-eventid is not initial.

          lv_eventid = |{ ls_keys-eventid alpha = in }|.

          select single nfa_id from znfa_header into @data(nfaidd) where event_no =  @lv_eventid
            and status ne 'CN'.


          if sy-subrc = 0.
            data : msg type bapi_msg.
            msg = |{ 'NFAID:-' && nfaidd && 'Already Created For This Event Number' }|.
            raise exception type /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
              exporting
                textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
                message = msg.
          endif.
          select from zrfq_header as d inner join
            zrfq_item as a on ( a~event_no = d~event_no
            and a~rfqid = d~rfqid )
            left outer join eban as b on ( b~banfn = a~prnumber and b~bnfpo = a~pritem )
            left outer join t001k as c on ( c~bwkey =  a~werks )
            fields
            a~event_no,
            a~rfqid,
            a~round,
            a~rfq_qty,
            a~currency,
            a~pritem,
            a~prnumber,
            a~lineitem,
            a~maktx,
            a~matnr,
            a~werks,
            a~purgroup,
            a~plant_add,
            a~prdate,
            a~uom,
            b~ekgrp,
            b~preis,
            b~peinh,
            b~waers,
            c~bukrs,
            d~rfqstatus
            where a~event_no = @lv_eventid
            and d~rfqstatus in ( 'Bid Complete' , 'Round Closed' )
            into table @data(it_d).

          sort it_d by round descending.
*          read table it_d into data(ww) index 1.
          loop at it_d into data(ww).
            select
              *
              from @it_d as a
              where a~event_no = @lv_eventid
                and round   = @ww-round
              into table @data(it_data).

            select
              event_no,
              unitamt,
              taxamt,
              totamt,
              currency,
              prnumber,
              prlineitem,
              lineitem,
              supplier,
              supplierref,
              suppliername
              from zrfq_item_bid_re as a
              into table @data(it_bidsub)
              where event_no =  @lv_eventid
                and supplierref = @ls_keys-supplierref.

            select
              b~bidstatus,
              a~event_no,
              a~prnumber,
              a~prlineitem,
              a~lineitem,
              a~supplier,
              a~supplierref,
              a~suppliername
              from zrfq_head_bid_re as b
              left outer join zrfq_item_bid_re as a
              on ( a~rfq_no = b~rfq_no
               and a~event_no = b~event_no  )
              where b~event_no = @lv_eventid
                and b~round = @ww-round
                and b~bidstatus = 'Quoted'
              into table @data(it_bidsub1) .

            ls_data-eventid = lv_eventid.
            ls_data-totalnfaamt = reduce dmbtr( init i type dmbtr for w in it_bidsub
                                            next i = i + w-totamt ).
            if it_bidsub1 is not initial.
              exit.
            endif.
          endloop.

          ls_Data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp =
          value #( for wa_data in it_data
                   ( eventid      = wa_data-event_no
                     rfqid        = wa_data-rfqid
                     rfq_qty      = wa_data-rfq_qty
                     material     = wa_data-matnr

                     material_des = wa_data-maktx
                     rfqstatus    = wa_data-rfqstatus
                     pritem       = wa_data-pritem
                     prnumber     = wa_data-prnumber
                     rfqlineitem  = wa_data-lineitem
                     uom          = wa_data-uom
                     currency     = wa_data-waers
                     totamtgst    = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = wa_data-prnumber prlineitem = wa_data-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-totamt optional )
                     award_supp   = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = wa_data-prnumber prlineitem = wa_data-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-supplier optional )
                     suppliername = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = wa_data-prnumber prlineitem = wa_data-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-suppliername optional )
                     supplierref  = value #( it_bidsub[ prnumber = wa_data-prnumber prlineitem = wa_data-pritem supplierref = ls_keys-supplierref ]-supplierref optional )

                   ) ).

          loop at ls_Data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp assigning field-symbol(<ff>) .
            read table it_bidsub1 into data(wa_bids) with key prnumber = <ff>-prnumber prlineitem = <ff>-pritem.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              <ff>-bidstatus = wa_bids-bidstatus.
            endif.

          endloop.

        endif.

        delete  ls_Data-nfaheadertonfaitemnp where  bidstatus ne 'Quoted'.

        select from zrfq_head_bid_re as a
                  inner join zrfq_item_bid_re as b on ( b~event_no =  a~event_no and
                                                        b~rfq_no   = a~rfq_no  and

        select max( a~round ) as round,a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
                  from zrfq_item_bid_re  as a inner join @lt_item as b on ( b~lineitem = a~lineitem and b~event_no = a~event_no )
                  group by a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
                   into table @data(it_max).

        select min( a~round ) as round,a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
          from zrfq_item_bid_re  as a inner join @lt_item as b on ( b~lineitem = a~lineitem and b~event_no = a~event_no )

          group by a~supplier,a~supplierref, a~lineitem,a~event_no
           into table @data(it_min).

        select distinct a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no, a~suppliername
        from zrfq_item_bid_re  as a inner join @lt_item as b on ( b~event_no = a~event_no and b~lineitem = a~lineitem )
         into table @data(it_supplier) .

        sort it_supplier by supplierref.
        sort it_bid by supplierref.
        loop at lt_item into data(wa_ven).
          loop at it_supplier into data(wa_supplier) where lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem.
            read table it_max into data(wa_max) with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                        event_no = wa_supplier-event_no .
            if sy-subrc eq 0.
              read table it_bid into data(WA_BID_max) with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                        event_no = wa_supplier-event_no round = wa_max-round.
              if sy-subrc eq 0.
                l_bid-finalquote = WA_BID_max-unitamt.
                l_bid-finalquotegst = wa_bid_max-taxamt.
              endif.
            endif.

            read table it_min into data(wa_min) with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                                      event_no = wa_supplier-event_no.
            if sy-subrc eq 0.
              read table it_bid into data(WA_BID_min) with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                        event_no = wa_supplier-event_no round = wa_min-round.
              if sy-subrc eq 0.
                l_bid-initialquote = WA_BID_min-unitamt.
              endif.
            endif.

            l_bid-vendorname = wa_supplier-supplierNAME.
            l_bid-supplierref = wa_supplier-supplierref.
            l_bid-eventid   = wa_supplier-event_no.
            l_bid-lineitem   = wa_supplier-lineitem.
            append l_bid to i_bid.
            clear : l_bid.
          endloop.
        endloop.

        sort i_bid.
        delete adjacent duplicates from i_bid comparing all fields. " Added by ALI on 30/07/26 because in Executive  tab Commercial Diff from Others (L1 vs L2) is getting doubled

        loop at i_bid into l_bid group by ( vendorname = l_bid-vendorname ) into data(groupkey).
          clear ls_rank.

          loop at group groupkey into data(ls_gp).
            ls_rank-vendorname = ls_gp-vendorname.
            ls_rank-tot = ls_gp-finalquote + ls_rank-tot + ls_gp-finalquotegst.
          endloop.
          append ls_rank to lt_rank.

        endloop.

        sort lt_rank by tot ascending.
        data(lv_rank) = 1.
        loop at lt_rank into ls_rank.
          ls_rank-ranking = |L{ lv_rank }|.
          if ls_rank-ranking = 'L1'.
            data(l1) = ls_rank-tot.
          endif.
          if ls_rank-ranking = 'L2'.
            data(l2) = ls_rank-tot.
          endif.

          commdif_l1l2 = l2 - l1.
          if  commdif_l1l2 lt 0.
            commdif_l1l2  = commdif_l1l2 * -1.
          endif.
          modify lt_rank from ls_rank.
          lv_rank = lv_rank + 1.
        endloop.

            a~rfq_qty,

            a~pritem,

            a~lineitem,
            a~maktx,

            a~uom,
            b~ekgrp,
            b~preis,

            b~waers,
            c~bukrs
            where event_no = @lv_eventid and
            round   = ( select max( round ) from zrfq_item where event_no = @lv_eventid )
            into table @it_data.

        select * from zrfq_item into table @lt_item where event_no = @lv_eventid." AND rfqid = @LS_KEYS-rfqid.

        data(countl) = lines( lt_item ).

        read table lt_item into data(wt_itm) index countl.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          data(rfq_num) = wt_itm-rfqid.
        endif.

        delete lt_item where rfqid ne rfq_num.
        clear : rfq_num.

        select from zrfq_head_bid_re as a
          inner join zrfq_item_bid_re as b on ( b~event_no =  a~event_no and
                                                b~rfq_no   = a~rfq_no  and
                                                b~quotationid  = a~quotationid )
          fields
          b~round,
          b~lineitem,
          b~supplier,
          b~supplierref,
          b~suppliername,
          b~unitamt,

            exporting

          endif.

        select ekko~ebeln,
              ekko~aedat,
              ekko~lifnr,
              ekpo~matnr,
              ekpo~werks
         into table @data(it_last)

         inner join ekpo
           on ekko~ebeln = ekpo~ebeln
         for all entries in @lt_item
         where ekpo~matnr = @lt_item-matnr
           and ekpo~werks = @lt_item-werks.

        sort it_last by matnr aedat descending.
        delete adjacent duplicates from it_last comparing matnr.

        select a~ebeln , a~lifnr , a~aedat , a~matnr , a~werks , sum( b~netwr ) as povalue , d~ort01
        from @it_last as a inner join ekpo as b on ( b~ebeln = a~ebeln and b~matnr = a~matnr and b~werks = a~werks )
          left outer join t001w as d on ( d~werks = b~werks )
          group by a~ebeln , a~lifnr , a~aedat , a~matnr , a~werks , d~ort01
          into table @data(it_lastpo).

        select max( a~round ) as round,a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
          from zrfq_item_bid_re  as a inner join @lt_item as b on ( b~lineitem = a~lineitem and b~event_no = a~event_no )
          group by a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
           into table @it_max.

        select min( a~round ) as round,a~supplier,a~supplierref,a~lineitem,a~event_no
          from zrfq_item_bid_re  as a inner join @lt_item as b on ( b~lineitem = a~lineitem and b~event_no = a~event_no )

          group by a~supplier,a~supplierref, a~lineitem,a~event_no
           into table @it_min.

         into table @it_supplier .

          loop at it_supplier into wa_supplier where lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem.
            read table it_max into wa_max with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                        event_no = wa_supplier-event_no .

                                                                      event_no = wa_supplier-event_no.
            if sy-subrc eq 0.
              read table it_bid into WA_BID_min with key lineitem = wa_ven-lineitem supplierref  = wa_supplier-supplierref
                                                        event_no = wa_supplier-event_no round = wa_min-round.
              if sy-subrc eq 0.
                l_bid-initialquote = WA_BID_min-unitamt.
              endif.
            endif.

            l_bid-vendorname = wa_supplier-supplierNAME.
            l_bid-eventid   = wa_supplier-event_no.
            l_bid-lineitem   = wa_supplier-lineitem.
            append l_bid to i_bid.
            clear : l_bid.
          endloop.
        endloop.

        append initial line to ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning field-symbol(<fs_line>).
        <fs_line>-deliveryloc = 'Total Price(Without GST)'.
        unassign <fs_line>.
        append initial line to ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>.
        <fs_line>-deliveryloc = 'Total Price(With GST)'.
        unassign <fs_line>.
        append initial line to ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>.
        <fs_line>-deliveryloc = 'Commercial Rank(with GST)'.

        data(ls_data3) = ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset[ 1 ].
        sort ls_data3-compsummtocompsumvennp by vendorname.
        if ls_data3-compsummtocompsumvennp is not initial.
          loop at ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset[ 1 ]-CompSummToCompSumVenNP into data(ls_data2).
            lv_amt = ls_data2-finalquote.
            lv_amt_gst = ls_data2-finalquote + ls_data2-finalquotegst.
            loop at ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset into ls_data3.
              if sy-tabix = 1.
                continue.
              endif.
              read table ls_data3-compsummtocompsumvennp into data(ls_amt_data)
                   with key vendorname = ls_data2-vendorname.
              if sy-subrc is initial.
                lv_amt = lv_amt + ls_amt_data-finalquote.
                lv_amt_gst = lv_amt_gst + ls_amt_data-finalquote + ls_amt_data-finalquotegst.
              endif.
            endloop.
            " WITHOUT GST
            read table ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>
                 with key deliveryloc = 'Total Price(Without GST)'.
            if sy-subrc is initial.
              append initial line to <fs_line>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning field-symbol(<fs_line1>).
              <fs_line1>-vendorname = ls_data2-vendorname.
              <fs_line1>-finalquote = lv_amt.
            endif.
            unassign <fs_line1>.
            " WITH GST
            read table ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>
                 with key deliveryloc = 'Total Price(With GST)'.
            if sy-subrc is initial.
              append initial line to <fs_line>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning <fs_line1>.
              <fs_line1>-vendorname = ls_data2-vendorname.
              <fs_line1>-finalquote = lv_amt_gst.
            endif.
            unassign <fs_line1>.
          endloop.
        endif.




        read table ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>
            with key deliveryloc = 'Total Price(With GST)'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          loop at <fs_line>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning <fs_line1>.
            clear ls_rank.
            ls_rank-vendorname = <fs_line1>-vendorname.
            ls_rank-tot = <fs_line1>-finalquote.
            append ls_rank to lt_rank.
          endloop.
        endif.
        sort lt_rank by tot ascending.
        lv_rank = 1.
        loop at lt_rank into ls_rank.
          ls_rank-ranking = |L{ lv_rank }|.
          if ls_rank-ranking = 'L1'.
            l1 = ls_rank-tot.
          endif.
          if ls_rank-ranking = 'L2'.
            l2 = ls_rank-tot.
          endif.

          commdif_l1l2 = l2 - l1.
          if  commdif_l1l2 lt 0.
            commdif_l1l2  = commdif_l1l2 * -1.
          endif.
          modify lt_rank from ls_rank.
          lv_rank = lv_rank + 1.
        endloop.


        read table ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning <fs_line>
            with key deliveryloc = 'Commercial Rank(with GST)'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          " structure create
          read table ls_data1-nfacomsummvenset assigning field-symbol(<fs_total>)
               with key deliveryloc = 'Total Price(With GST)'.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            loop at <fs_total>-compsummtocompsumvennp into data(ls_temp).
              append initial line to <fs_line>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning <fs_line1>.
              <fs_line1>-vendorname = ls_temp-vendorname.
            endloop.
          endif.

          loop at <fs_line>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning <fs_line1>.
            read table lt_rank into ls_rank
                 with key vendorname = <fs_line1>-vendorname.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              <fs_line1>-finalquote = ls_rank-ranking.
            endif.
          endloop.
        endif.


        loop at ls_data1-nfadetheadtoproposedvennp assigning field-symbol(<nfaven>).
          if <nfaven>-totalvenquoted = 1.
            <nfaven>-commdiff = 0.
          else.
            <nfaven>-commdiff = commdif_l1l2.
          endif.
          loop at <nfaven>-proposedventoproposedvenitemnp assigning field-symbol(<nfapro>).
            read table lt_rank into ls_rank with key vendorname = <nfapro>-awardedsupp.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              <nfapro>-commercialrank = ls_rank-ranking.
            endif.

          endloop.
        endloop.

          case ls_ROLES-description.

          <fmt_ditm>-budgetamt = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_ditm>-budgetamt ).
          <fmt_ditm>-nfaamount = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_ditm>-nfaamount ).
        endloop.

        loop at ls_data1-nfadetheadtoproposedvennp assigning field-symbol(<fmt_pven>).
          <fmt_pven>-totalvenquoted = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_pven>-totalvenquoted ).
          <fmt_pven>-commdiff       = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_pven>-commdiff ).

        endloop.

          <fmt_csum>-quantity    = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_csum>-quantity ).
          <fmt_csum>-comm_rank   = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_csum>-comm_rank ).

          loop at <fmt_csum>-compsummtocompsumvennp assigning field-symbol(<fmt_cven>).
            <fmt_cven>-initialquote = me->format_amount( iv_amount = <fmt_cven>-initialquote ).
            " On the rank row FinalQuote carries L1/L2 - must stay as is

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_STREAM
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [<---] ER_STREAM                      TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~get_stream.

    data: ls_key_tab      type /iwbep/s_mgw_name_value_pair,
          pritmkey        type drad-objky,
          docid           type doknr,
          lv_file_name    type char200,
          ls_stream       type ty_s_media_resource,
          ls_header       type ihttpnvp,
          lv_name_utf8    type string,
          lv_filename_str type string,

          lv_KEY          type eban-purchasereqnitemuniqueid,
          lv_id           type   sdok_phid.



    case iv_entity_set_name.
      when  'GetGnfaAttachmentSet'.
        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'NFAID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          data(nfaid) = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          docid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        select single from zrfq_attachment
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where nfaid = @nfaid and doknr = @docid into @data(ls_rfqatt).

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).


      when 'GetBussinessCaseAttachSet'.
        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'NFAID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          nfaid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          docid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        select single from zrfq_attachment
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where nfaid = @nfaid and doknr = @docid into @ls_rfqatt.

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).


      when 'GetCostBenAttachmentSet'.
        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'NFAID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          nfaid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          docid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        select single from zrfq_attachment
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where nfaid = @nfaid and doknr = @docid into @ls_rfqatt.

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).

      when 'GetPRAttachmentSet'.


        read table  it_key_tab into data(ls_cor) with key name = 'Key'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          lv_KEY = ls_cor-value.
          clear : ls_cor.
        endif.

        read table  it_key_tab into ls_cor with key name = 'ID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          lv_id = ls_cor-value.
          clear : ls_cor.
        endif.


        select single from drad
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where objky = @lv_key  into @ls_rfqatt.

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
                objid     = lv_ID
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).


      when  'GetRFQAttachmentSet'.
        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'RFQID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          data(rfqid) = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          docid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        select single from zrfq_attachment
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where rfqid = @rfqid and doknr = @docid into corresponding fields of @ls_rfqatt.

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).
      when 'GetBidAttachmentSet'.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'RFQID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          rfqid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          docid = ls_key_tab-value.
        endif.

*        read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'QutationID'.
*        if sy-subrc = 0.
*          data(quoID) = ls_key_tab-value.
*        endif.

        select single from zrfq_attachment
          fields
               dokar,
               doknr,
               dokvr,
               doktl
          where rfqid = @rfqid
*          and quotationid = @quoid
          and
          doknr = @docid into @data(LS_bidATT).

        try.
            call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_bidatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_bidatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_bidatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_bidatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

        ls_header-name = 'Content-Disposition'.
        lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
        lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped = lv_filename_str ).
        replace all occurrences of '%2e' in lv_name_utf8 with '.'.
        concatenate 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' lv_name_utf8 into ls_header-value.
        set_header( is_header = ls_header ).

    endcase.
    copy_data_to_ref( exporting is_data = ls_stream
                      changing  cr_data = er_stream ).
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->APPROVALDETAILSS_GET_ENTITY

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->BIDATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_BIDATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method BIDATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET.

data : r_quoid  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-QUOTATIONID,
           l_quoid  like line of r_quoid,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset,
           r_rfqid  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-rfqid,
           l_rfqid  like line of r_rfqid.

*    try.
*            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'QutationID' ].
*           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
*            l_quoid-low = ls_selopt-low.
*            l_quoid-sign   = 'I'.
*            l_quoid-option = 'EQ'.
*            append  l_quoid to r_quoid.
*            clear : ls_selopt.
*            ENDLOOP.
*            clear : ls_filter.
*          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
*        endtry.

try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'RFQID' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_rfqid-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_rfqid-sign   = 'I'.
            l_rfqid-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_rfqid to r_rfqid.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.


SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT

  FIELDS
  RFQID,
 QUOTATIONID,
  FILE_NAME,
  UPLOADED_BY,
  DOKNR,
  Specification,
  file_type,
  SENDTOSUPPLIER
  WHERE RFQID IN @r_rfqid
  "and QUOTATIONID in @r_quoid
  and  attachmenttype = 'BID'
  INTO  TABLE @DATA(IT_DATA).

et_entityset =  VALUE #( for LS_DATA IN IT_DATA INDEX INTO IND (

                         SRNO = IND
                         Specification = ls_data-Specification
                         FileName = ls_data-FILE_NAME
                         FileType = ls_data-file_type
                         DocID  = ls_data-DOKNR
                         SendToSupplier = ls_data-SENDTOSUPPLIER
                         RFQID = ls_data-rfqid

                         ) ).


  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->CANCELNFASET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TS_CANCELNFA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method CANCELNFASET_UPDATE_ENTITY.

data(lv_nfaid) = it_key_tab[ name = 'NFAID' ]-value.

data : ls_data type ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_mpc=>ts_cancelnfa.

 io_data_provider->read_entry_data(
      importing
        es_data = ls_data
    ).


 if ls_data-status = 'CN'.

   UPDATE ZNFA_HEADER SET status = 'CN'
   WHERE nfa_id = @lv_nfaid.
    if sy-subrc is initial.
        commit work.
      endif.
   ENDIF.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->EVENTIDF4SET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_EVENTIDF4
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method eventidf4set_get_entityset.
    data: lv_top  type string,
          lv_skip type i,
          eventno type zrfq_header-event_no.
**- Get number of records requested
    clear: lv_top,lv_skip.
    lv_top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).

**- Get number of lines that should be skipped
    lv_skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).
    lv_top = lv_top + lv_skip.

    eventno = iv_search_string.
    EVENTNO = |%{ EVENTNO }%|.
    if eventno  is initial.
      select  distinct event_no as EventID
        from zrfq_header into table @et_entityset.
    else.
      select  distinct event_no as EventID
      from zrfq_header where event_no like @eventno  into table @et_entityset.
    endif.
    if et_entityset is not initial.
      SORT et_entityset by eventid DESCENDING.
      loop at et_entityset assigning field-symbol(<fs_entityset>).
        <fs_entityset>-eventid = |{ <fs_entityset>-eventid alpha = out }|.
      endloop.
    endif.
**- Skip/Top Logic to Restrict No Of Records
    if lv_skip is not initial and lv_skip <> '0'.
      delete et_entityset from 1 to lv_skip.
    endif.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->FORMAT_AMOUNT
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_AMOUNT                      TYPE        STRING
* | [<-()] RV_TEXT                        TYPE        STRING
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method FORMAT_AMOUNT.
    DATA: lv_work TYPE string,
          lv_int  TYPE string,
          lv_dec  TYPE string,
          lv_sign TYPE string,
          lv_rest TYPE string,
          lt_grp  TYPE TABLE OF string.

    lv_work = iv_amount.
    CONDENSE lv_work NO-GAPS.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ',' IN lv_work WITH ''.

    IF lv_work CS '-'.
      lv_sign = '-'.
      REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '-' IN lv_work WITH ''.
    ENDIF.

    SPLIT lv_work AT '.' INTO lv_int lv_dec.

    SHIFT lv_int LEFT DELETING LEADING '0'.
    IF lv_int IS INITIAL.
      lv_int = '0'.
    ENDIF.

    lv_dec = |{ lv_dec }00|.
    lv_dec = lv_dec(2).

    DATA(lv_len) = strlen( lv_int ).

    IF lv_len <= 3.
      rv_text = lv_int.
    ELSE.
      DATA(lv_off) = lv_len - 3.
      APPEND lv_int+lv_off(3) TO lt_grp.
      lv_rest = lv_int(lv_off).

      WHILE lv_rest IS NOT INITIAL.
        DATA(lv_rlen) = strlen( lv_rest ).
        IF lv_rlen <= 2.
          INSERT lv_rest INTO lt_grp INDEX 1.
          CLEAR lv_rest.
        ELSE.
          DATA(lv_roff) = lv_rlen - 2.
          INSERT lv_rest+lv_roff(2) INTO lt_grp INDEX 1.
          lv_rest = lv_rest(lv_roff).
        ENDIF.
      ENDWHILE.

      CONCATENATE LINES OF lt_grp INTO rv_text SEPARATED BY ','.
    ENDIF.

    rv_text = |{ lv_sign }{ rv_text }.{ lv_dec }|.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->GETGLMAPPINGSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_GETGLMAPPING
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method getglmappingset_get_entityset.

    if line_exists( it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ] ).
      data(lv_nfaid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    endif.

    select e~sakto
      into table @data(lt_sakto)
      from znfa_header as h
      inner join zrfq_item as r
        on r~event_no = h~event_no
      inner join ebkn as e
        on e~banfn = r~prnumber
       and e~bnfpo = r~pritem
     where h~nfa_id = @lv_nfaid.

    data: it_list type table of abaplist.

    SUBMIT gpe381prrwwfxd8mghe37mhg991200
  VIA SELECTION-SCREEN
  AND RETURN.

*    submit gpe381prrwwfxd8mghe37mhg991200
*    WITH $4FFIKRS = 'AEPC'
*    WITH $0SBUCAT = '9F'
*    WITH $4FFYEAR = '2025'
*      exporting list to memory
*      and return.
*
*    " Retrieve the data from memory
*    call function 'LIST_FROM_MEMORY'
*      tables
*        listobject = it_list
*      exceptions
*        not_found  = 1
*        others     = 2.

  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->GETNFADATASET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_GETNFADATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method getnfadataset_get_entityset.
    data : lv_nfaid type char10.

    if iv_filter_string is not initial.
      find regex `'([^']+)'` in iv_filter_string submatches data(lv_value).
      lv_nfaid = lv_value.
      select
        a~prnumber,
        a~pritem,
        a~matnr,
        a~maktx,
        a~awardedsupplier,
        a~suppliername,
        a~nfaamt,
        a~baseamt,
        a~taxamt,
        a~totunitprice,
        a~taxcode,
        a~bukrs,
        b~ebeln,
        D~BATXT
        from zgnfa_item as a LEFT OUTER join eban as b on ( b~banfn = a~prnumber and b~bnfpo = a~pritem )
        LEFT outer join ekko as c on ( c~ebeln = b~ebeln )
        LEFT OUTER join T161T as d on ( d~bsart = c~bsart and d~spras = 'E' )
        into table @data(lt_data)
        where nfaid = @lv_nfaid.
      if lt_data is not initial.
        sort lt_data.
        loop at lt_data into data(ls_data).
          append value #(
       prnumber     = ls_data-prnumber
       pritem       = ls_data-pritem
       material     = ls_data-matnr
       materialdesc = ls_data-maktx
       baseamt      = ls_Data-baseamt
       taxamt           = ls_data-taxamt
       taxcode           = ls_data-taxcode
       totalunitprice           = ls_data-totunitprice
       vendor       = ls_data-awardedsupplier
       companycode       = ls_data-bukrs
       vendorname   = ls_data-suppliername
       nfaamount    = ls_data-nfaamt  ponumber = ls_data-ebeln
       DOCTYPE       = LS_DATA-BATXT ) to et_entityset.
        endloop.
      endif.
    endif.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->GETPRATTACHMENTS_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->GNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_GNFAATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method GNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET.

data : r_NFAID  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-NFAID,
           l_NFAID  like line of r_NFAID,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset.

    try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_NFAid-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_NFAid-sign   = 'I'.
            l_NFAid-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_NFAid to r_NFAid.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.


SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT

  FIELDS
  RFQID,
  NFAID,
  FILE_NAME,
  UPLOADED_BY,
  DOKNR,
  Specification,
  file_type,
  SENDTOSUPPLIER,
  SENDTOPO
  WHERE NFAID IN @r_NFAid and attachmenttype = 'GNFA'
  INTO  TABLE @DATA(IT_DATA).

et_entityset =  VALUE #( for LS_DATA IN IT_DATA INDEX INTO IND (

                         SRNO = IND
                         Specification = ls_data-Specification
                         FileName = ls_data-FILE_NAME
                         FileType = ls_data-file_type
                         DocID  = ls_data-DOKNR
                         SendToSupplier = ls_data-SENDTOSUPPLIER
                         RFQID = ls_data-rfqid
                         SENDTOPO = LS_DATA-sendtopo
                         NFAID  = LS_DATA-nfaid

                         ) ).


  endmethod.

* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TS_HISTORY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT

**try.

*  EXPORTING
*    IV_ENTITY_NAME          =

*    IV_SOURCE_NAME          =

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->HISTORYSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_HISTORY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method HISTORYSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
 data(lv_nfaid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    if lv_nfaid is not initial.
      select
        nfaid,
        alevel as level,
        action,
        remark,
         cast( edate as char( 8 ) ) as date,
         cast( etime as char( 8 ) ) as time
        from zgnfa_history
        into table @et_entityset
        where nfaid = @lv_nfaid.
      if et_entityset is not initial.
        loop at et_entityset assigning field-symbol(<ls_entityset>).
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'A'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = 'Approved'.
          endif.
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'R'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = 'Rejected'.
          endif.
          <ls_entityset>-date = |{ <ls_entityset>-date+4(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+6(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+0(4) }|.
          <ls_entityset>-time = |{ <ls_entityset>-time+0(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+2(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+4(2) }|.
        endloop.
        sort et_entityset by date ascending time ascending.
      endif.
    endif.
  endmethod.
