* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_NFADETHEADER
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method nfadetheaderset_get_entityset.
    types: begin of ty_nfa,
             nfatype     type zde_nfatype,
             gencat      type zde_gencat,
             ekgrp       type ekgrp,
             opex_capex  type zde_astcls,
             contractval type zde_conval,
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*             nfaamt      type zde_nfaamout,
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
           end of ty_nfa.
    data : lr_nfaid      type range of char10,
           lv_level      type char10,
           lv_apppending type string,
           lo_facade     type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid    type string,
           lv_logintype  type char1,
           lv_userrole   type char1,
           lv_bukrs      type bukrs,
           lr_eventid    type range of zgnfa_item-eventid,
           gt_nfa        type standard table of ty_nfa,
           gs_nfa        type ty_nfa.
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*           lv_amt        type zde_nfaamout.
    data : ls_approval   type zgnfa_approval,
           lv_appfound   type abap_bool.
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*

    read table it_filter_select_options transporting no fields with key property = 'NFAID'.
    if sy-subrc is initial and it_filter_select_options is not initial.
      lr_nfaid = corresponding #( it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options ) .
    endif.

    read table it_filter_select_options transporting no fields with key property = 'EventID'.
    if sy-subrc is initial and it_filter_select_options is not initial.
      lr_eventid = corresponding #( it_filter_select_options[ property = 'EventID' ]-select_options ) .
    endif.

    loop at lr_eventid assigning field-symbol(<ev>).
      <ev>-low = |{ <ev>-low alpha = in }|.

    endloop.

**-- Get Request Header Object Reference
    lo_facade ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

**-- Get Parameters
    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).

    if lv_loginid is not initial.
      select single * from zapp_roles into @data(ls_role) where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'ADMIN' and application = 'APP.11'.
      if sy-subrc is not initial.
        select single * from zapp_roles into @ls_role where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'BUYER' and application = 'APP.11'.
        if sy-subrc is not initial.

          select single * from zapp_roles into @ls_role
             where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'APPROVER' and application = 'APP.11'.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            data(approver) = 'X'.
            translate lv_loginid to upper case.
            select single id, name from zrfq_user_master into @data(ls_ID) where id = @lv_loginid .
          endif.

        else.
          data(buyerrole) = 'X'.
          translate lv_loginid to upper case.
          select single id, name from zrfq_user_master into @ls_ID where id = @lv_loginid.
        endif.
      else.
        data(admin) = 'X'.
      endif.
    endif.



    select
      from znfa_header as a left outer join
      zrfq_header as b on ( b~event_no = a~event_no )
      left outer  join zgnfa_item  as c on ( c~eventid = a~event_no )
      left outer join eban as d on ( d~banfn = c~prnumber and d~bnfpo = c~pritem )
      left outer join t024 as e1 on ( e1~ekgrp = a~ekgrp )
      left outer join zrfq_user_master as e on ( e~id = a~buyer and e~usertype = 'BUYER' )



           fields
           distinct
      a~nfa_id,
      a~event_no,
      a~buyer,
      a~nfa_type,
      a~grp_detail,
      a~scope,
      a~scope2,
      a~ekgrp,
      cast( a~opex_capex as char( 30 ) ) as opex_capex,
      a~tot_bud_amt,
      a~tot_nfa_amt,
      a~currency,
      a~alevel,
      a~status,
      a~draft,
      a~createdon,
      a~createdat,
      b~gen_category,
      c~prnumber,
      c~pritem,
      d~ebeln,
      e~name as buyername,
      e1~eknam,
      ' ' as querysolve,
      ' ' as querychk
      where a~nfa_id in @lr_nfaid and a~event_no in @lr_eventid
*      and a~buyer = @lv_loginid
      into table @data(lt_GNFA).

    sort lt_gnfa  by nfa_id.
    delete adjacent duplicates from lt_gnfa comparing nfa_id.

    types : begin of ty_q,
              nfaid      type zgnfa_query_tab-nfaid,
              answer     type zgnfa_query_tab-answer,
              querychk   type zgnfa_query_tab-querychk,
              querysolve type char1,
            end of ty_Q.

    data :  lv_flag  type abap_bool.


    loop at lt_gnfa assigning field-symbol(<fs_gnfa>).
      select *
        into table @data(lt_query)
        from zgnfa_query_tab
        where nfaid = @<fs_gnfa>-nfa_id.
      if lt_query is initial.
        clear: <fs_gnfa>-querysolve,
     <fs_gnfa>-querychk.
      else.
        lv_flag = abap_true.
        loop at lt_query into data(ls_query).
          if ls_query-answer is initial.
            lv_flag = abap_false.
            exit.
          endif.
        endloop.
        if lv_flag = abap_true.
          <fs_gnfa>-querysolve = 'X'.
          clear <fs_gnfa>-querychk.
        else.
          clear <fs_gnfa>-querysolve.
          <fs_gnfa>-querychk = 'X'.
        endif.
      endif.
    endloop.



*
*  SELECT  NFAID ,QUERYCHK , ANSWER FROM ZGNFA_QUERY_TAB
**  FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_GNFA
*  WHERE NFAID = @WGNFA-nfa_id
*  INTO TABLE @DATA(NFA_QUERY).
*  IF LINE_EXISTS( NFA_QUERY[ ANSWER = ' ' ] ).
*    LOOP AT NFA_QUERY ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<FD>).
*      <FD>-querychk = 'X'.
*
*      ENDLOOP.
*    ELSE.
*       LOOP AT NFA_QUERY ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<FD1>).
*      <FD1>-querychk = ' '.
*
*      ENDLOOP.
*
*   ENDIF.
*
*
*  ENDLOOP.



    if lt_GNFA is not initial.
      sort lt_GNFA by nfa_id descending.
      select * from zdepartment_det into table @data(lt_DEPARTMENT_DET) for all entries in @lt_GNFA where pur_grp = @lt_GNFA-ekgrp.

      loop at lt_GNFA into data(ls_GNFA).
        select
          sum( totamt ) as totamt
          from zgnfa_item
          into  @data(lv_totamt)
          where eventid = @ls_GNFA-event_no.

        select single
          gen_category
          from zrfq_header
          into @data(lv_gen_category)
          where event_no = @ls_GNFA-event_no.

        append value #( nfaid           = ls_GNFA-nfa_id PurCHASEGrp  = ls_GNFA-ekgrp    buyer         = ls_GNFA-buyername status = ls_GNFA-status
                        AppPending      = lv_apppending  eventid      = ls_gnfa-event_no purchaseorder = ls_gnfa-ebeln
                        purchasegrpdesc = ls_gnfa-eknam  pendingsince = lv_date          scope         = |{ ls_gnfa-scope }{ ls_gnfa-scope2 }|
                        querychk        = ls_GNFA-querychk
                        CreationDate    = lv_create
                        querysolve      = ls_GNFA-querysolve


        ) to et_entityset.
        clear :lv_apppending ,ls_USER_MASTER.
      endloop.
    endif.

    if APPROVEr = 'X'.
      delete et_entityset where status ne 'UA'.
    endif.

    clear : buyerrole , approver .
*, LV_FLAG.
    sort et_entityset by nfaid descending.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->NFAID_F4SET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_NFAID_F4
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method nfaid_f4set_get_entityset.
    data: lv_top  type string,
          lv_skip type i,
          nfaid   type string.
**- Get number of records requested
    clear: lv_top,lv_skip.
    lv_top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).

**- Get number of lines that should be skipped
    lv_skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).
    lv_top = lv_top + lv_skip.

    nfaid =  |%{ iv_search_string }%|.
    if nfaid is initial.
      select from znfa_header
        fields distinct  nfa_id as nfaid
        where draft is initial
        into table @et_entityset .
    else.
      select from znfa_header
       fields distinct  nfa_id as nfaid
        where nfa_id like @nfaid
        and draft is initial
       into table @et_entityset.
    endif.
    SORT et_entityset by nfaid DESCENDING.
    loop at et_entityset assigning field-symbol(<fs_entityset>).
      <fs_entityset>-nfaid = |{ <fs_entityset>-nfaid alpha = out }|.
    endloop.
**- Skip/Top Logic to Restrict No Of Records
    if lv_skip is not initial and lv_skip <> '0'.
      delete et_entityset from 1 to lv_skip.
    endif.
  endmethod.

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TS_NFAVWITEM
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method NFAVWITEMSET_GET_ENTITY.
**try.
*CALL METHOD SUPER->NFAVWITEMSET_GET_ENTITY
*  EXPORTING
*    IV_ENTITY_NAME          =
*    IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME      =

**    es_response_context     =
*    .

  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->PO_CREATE
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] NFAID                          TYPE        ZDE_NFAID
* | [--->] EVENTID                        TYPE        ZDE_EVENTNO
* | [--->] DOCTYPE                        TYPE        CHAR4
* | [--->] I_DATA                         TYPE        TT_DATA
* | [<---] RETURN                         TYPE        TT_RETURN
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method po_create.

    types: begin of ty_head,
             doctype         type char4,
             awardedsupplier type zgnfa_item-awardedsupplier,
             prnumber        type zgnfa_item-prnumber,
             pritem          type zgnfa_item-pritem,
             bukrs           type zgnfa_item-bukrs,
             nfaid           type zgnfa_item-nfaid,
             werks           type zgnfa_item-werks,
             currency        type zgnfa_item-currency,
             lineitem        type zgnfa_item-lineitem,
             matnr           type zgnfa_item-matnr,
             maktx           type zgnfa_item-maktx,
             rfq_qty         type zgnfa_item-rfq_qty,
             uom             type zgnfa_item-uom,
             eventid         type zgnfa_item-eventid,
             nfaamt          type zgnfa_item-nfaamt,
             baseamt         type zgnfa_item-baseamt,
             totunitprice    type zgnfa_item-totunitprice,
             taxcode         type zgnfa_item-taxcode,
           end of ty_head.
    data: ls_poheader       type bapimepoheader,
          ls_poheaderx      type bapimepoheaderx,
          lt_poitems        type standard table of bapimepoitem with default key,
          ls_poitems        type  bapimepoitem,
          lt_poitemsx       type standard table of bapimepoitemx with default key,
          ls_poitemsx       type  bapimepoitemx,
          lt_poschedules    type standard table of bapimeposchedule with default key,
          lS_poschedules    type  bapimeposchedule,
          lt_poschedulesx   type standard table of bapimeposchedulx with default key,
          lS_poschedulesx   type  bapimeposchedulx,
          lt_poaccount      type standard table of bapimepoaccount with default key,
          lt_poaccountx     type standard table of bapimepoaccountx with default key,
          lt_POCOND         type standard table of bapimepocond with default key,
          ls_POCOND         type bapimepocond,
          lt_POCONDx        type standard table of BAPIMEPOCONDx with default key,
          ls_POCONDx        type  BAPIMEPOCONDx,
          lt_return         type standard table of bapiret2 with default key,
          lv_ponumber       type bapimepoheader-po_number,
          lv_message        type  string,
          lt_head           type table of ty_head,
          ls_head           type ty_head,
          lt_extensionin    type standard table of bapiparex with default key,
          lt_poservices     type standard table of bapiesllc with default key,
          ls_poservices     type bapiesllc,
          ls_ext            type bapiparex,
          ls_extx           type bapiparex,
          ls_ci_ekko        type ci_ekkodb,
          ls_te_item        type bapi_te_mepoitem,
          ls_te_itemx       type bapi_te_mepoitemx,
          ls_extensionin    type bapiparex,
          lt_poservicevalue type table of bapiesklc,
          ls_poservicevalue type bapiesklc.


    select
      prnumber,
      pritem,
      awardedsupplier,
      bukrs,
      nfaid,
      werks,
      currency,
      lineitem,
      matnr,
      maktx,
      rfq_qty,
      uom,
      eventid,
      nfaamt,
      baseamt,
      totunitprice,
      taxcode
      from zgnfa_item
      into table @data(lt_header)
      where nfaid = @nfaid
        and eventid = @eventid.
    if lt_header is not initial.
      select
        prnumber,
        pritem,
        purgroup
        from zrfq_prdashboard
        for all entries in @lt_header
        where prnumber = @lt_header-prnumber
        and pritem = @lt_header-pritem
        into table @data(lt_purgroup).

      select
        event_no,
        unitamt,
        taxcode
        from zrfq_item_bid_re
        into table @data(lt_item)
        for all entries in @lt_header
        where  event_no = @lt_header-eventid.

      select *
           from zrfq_itm_bid_srv
           into table @data(lt_bid_srv)
           for all entries in @lt_header
           where  event_no = @lt_header-eventid.
    endif.

    select single
      del_term,
      paymentterm
      from zrfq_header
      into @data(ls_header4)
      where event_no = @eventid.


**    "----------------------------
**    " 1) HEADER
**    "----------------------------

    loop at lt_header into data(ls_header2) .
      read table i_data into data(ls_data2) with key prnumber = ls_header2-prnumber pritem = ls_header2-pritem.
      move-corresponding ls_header2 to ls_head.
      ls_head-doctype = ls_data2-doctype.
      append ls_head to lt_head.
    endloop.

    loop at lt_head into data(ls_header1)." with key lifnr = ls_header-awardedsupplier bukrs = ls_header-bukrs.
      data(ls_header) = ls_header1.
      select single ebeln from eban into @data(lv_po) where banfn = @ls_header-prnumber and bnfpo = @ls_header-pritem.
      if lv_po is not initial.
        ls_header-awardedsupplier = |{ ls_header-awardedsupplier alpha = in }|.
        select single name1 from lfa1 where lifnr = @ls_header-awardedsupplier into @data(lv_name1).
        ls_header-awardedsupplier = |{ ls_header-awardedsupplier alpha = out }|.
        append value #(
        nfaid        = |{ nfaid alpha = out }|
        prnumber     = ls_header-prnumber
        pritem       = ls_header-pritem
        material     = ls_header-matnr
        materialdesc = ls_header-maktx
        vendor       = ls_header-awardedsupplier
        vendorname   = lv_name1
        nfaamount    = ls_header-nfaamt
        doctype      = ls_poheader-doc_type
        companycode  = ls_header-bukrs
        ponumber     = lv_po
        Message      = 'PO already created' ) to return.
        clear : lv_po, ls_header,lv_name1.
        continue.
      endif.


      ls_poheader-doc_type = ls_header-doctype.
      ls_poheader-vendor = |{ ls_header-awardedsupplier alpha = in }|.
      select single ekorg from t024w into @data(lv_EKORG) where werks = @ls_header-werks.
      if lv_EKORG is not initial.
        ls_poheader-purch_org = lv_EKORG.
      endif.
      read table lt_purgroup into data(ls_purgroup) with key prnumber = ls_header-prnumber pritem = ls_header-pritem.
      if sy-subrc is initial.
        ls_poheader-pur_group = ls_purgroup-purgroup.
      endif.
      ls_poheader-our_ref  = ls_header-nfaid.
      ls_poheader-comp_code  = ls_header-bukrs.
      ls_poheader-currency   = ls_header-currency.
      ls_poheader-pmnttrms   = ls_header4-paymentterm.
      ls_poheader-vper_start = sy-datum.

      data: lv_date     type sy-datum,
            lv_new_date type sy-datum.

      lv_date = sy-datum.


      call function 'RP_CALC_DATE_IN_INTERVAL'
        exporting
          date      = lv_date
          years     = 1
          months    = 0
          days      = 0
        importing
          calc_date = lv_new_date.

      ls_poheader-vper_end   = lv_new_date.
      ls_poheader-incoterms1 = ls_header4-del_term.
      select single bezei from tinct into @data(lv_BEZEI) where inco1 = @ls_header4-del_term and spras = 'E'.
      if lv_BEZEI is not initial.
        ls_poheader-incoterms2 = lv_BEZEI.
      endif.

      ls_poheaderx-incoterms1 = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-incoterms2 = abap_true.
      ls_poheader-our_ref     = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-vper_start = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-vper_end   = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-doc_type   = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-vendor     = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-purch_org  = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-pur_group  = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-comp_code  = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-currency   = abap_true.
      ls_poheaderx-pmnttrms   = abap_true.

**    "----------------------------
**    " 2) ITEM (example: one stock item)
**    "----------------------------
      ls_poitems-po_item   = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_poitems-material  = |{ ls_header-matnr alpha = in }|.
      ls_poitems-plant     = ls_header-werks.
      ls_poitems-quantity  = ls_header-rfq_qty.
      ls_poitems-preq_no   = ls_header-prnumber.
      ls_poitems-preq_item = ls_header-pritem.
      ls_poitems-net_price = ls_header-totunitprice.
      ls_poitems-tax_code  = ls_header-taxcode.
      ls_poitems-pckg_no  = 000000001. "" Package NO.
      if ls_header-matnr is initial.
        ls_poitems-item_cat  = 'D'.
      endif.
      select single matkl , knttp, extstoragelocationforpurg , kostl , sakto  from eban into @data(ls_eban) where banfn = @ls_header-prnumber and bnfpo = @ls_header-pritem.
      select single kostl , sakto  from ebkn into @data(ls_ebkn) where banfn = @ls_header-prnumber and bnfpo = @ls_header-pritem.
      if sy-subrc is initial.
        ls_poitems-stge_loc = ls_eban-extstoragelocationforpurg.
        ls_poitems-matl_group = ls_eban-matkl.
        ls_poitems-acctasscat  = ls_eban-knttp.
      endif.

      ls_poitemsx-po_item   = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_poitemsx-po_itemx  = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-material  = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-plant     = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-quantity  = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-preq_no   = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-preq_item = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-net_price = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-tax_code  = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-pckg_no = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-item_cat  = abap_true.
      ls_poitemsx-acctasscat  = abap_true.
      append ls_poitems  to lt_poitems.
      append ls_poitemsx  to lt_poitemsx.

      clear ls_te_item.
      ls_te_item-po_item  = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_te_item-zgrn_uni = 'DUMMY00001'.
      ls_te_item-zpo_uni = 'DUMMY00001'.

      clear ls_extensionin.
      ls_extensionin-structure  = 'BAPI_TE_MEPOITEM'.
      ls_extensionin-valuepart1 = ls_te_item.
      append ls_extensionin to lt_extensionin.

      clear ls_te_itemx.
      ls_te_itemx-po_item  = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_te_itemx-zgrn_uni = abap_true.
      ls_te_itemx-zpo_uni = abap_true.

      clear ls_extensionin.
      ls_extensionin-structure  = 'BAPI_TE_MEPOITEMX'.
      ls_extensionin-valuepart1 = ls_te_itemx.
      append ls_extensionin to lt_extensionin.

      ls_poschedules-po_item        = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_poschedules-delivery_date  = sy-datum.
      ls_poschedulesx-po_item       = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_poschedulesx-po_itemx      = abap_true.
      ls_poschedulesx-delivery_date = abap_true.
      append lS_poschedules  to lt_poschedules.
      append lS_poschedulesX to lt_poschedulesx.


      ls_POCOND-itm_number = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_POCOND-cond_type = 'PB00'.
      ls_POCOND-cond_value = ls_header-totunitprice.
      ls_POCOND-currency = ls_header-currency.
      ls_POCOND-change_id = 'U'.

      append ls_POCOND to lt_POCOND.
      clear ls_POCOND.

      ls_POCONDx-itm_number = ls_header-lineitem.
      ls_POCONDx-cond_type = 'X'.
      ls_POCONDx-cond_value = 'X'.
      ls_POCONDx-currency = 'X'.
      ls_POCONDx-change_id = 'X'.

      append ls_POCONDx to lt_POCONDx.
      clear ls_POCONDx.

      clear ls_ci_ekko.
      ls_ci_ekko-zuid = 'L08'.

      clear ls_ext.
      ls_ext-structure = 'BAPI_TE_MEPOHEADER'.
      ls_ext-valuepart1 = ls_ci_ekko.
      append ls_ext to lt_extensionin.


      data: ls_headerx type bapi_te_mepoheaderx.

      clear ls_headerx.
      ls_headerx-zuid = 'X'.

      clear ls_ext.
      ls_ext-structure  = 'BAPI_TE_MEPOHEADERX'.
      ls_ext-valuepart1 = ls_headerx.

      append ls_ext to lt_extensionin.



      data lv_serial_no type bapiesklc-serial_no.

      """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Po Account data fill
      append value bapimepoaccount(
        po_item    = ls_header-lineitem
        serial_no  = lv_serial_no + 1
        gl_account = ls_ebkn-sakto
        costcenter = ls_ebkn-kostl
        funds_ctr  = ls_ebkn-kostl ) to lt_poaccount.
      """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Po Account data fill

      """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Po Account data fill
      append value bapimepoaccountx(
        po_item    = ls_header-lineitem
        serial_no  = lv_serial_no
        gl_account = 'X'
        costcenter = 'X'
        funds_ctr  = 'X' ) to lt_poaccountx.
      """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Po Account data fill

      ls_poservices-pckg_no    = ls_poservices-subpckg_no + 000000001. "" Package NO
      ls_poservices-outl_ind = 'X'.
      ls_poservices-ext_line = '0000000000'.
      ls_poservices-line_no =  '0000000001'.
      ls_poservices-subpckg_no = ls_poservices-pckg_no + 000000001.
      append ls_poservices to lt_poservices.
      clear : ls_poservices-outl_ind.

      loop at lt_bid_srv into data(ls_bid_srv) where event_no = ls_header-eventid.
        if ls_bid_srv is not initial.
          ls_poservices-line_no =  ls_poservices-line_no + 0000000001.
          ls_poservices-ext_line =  ls_poservices-ext_line + 0000000001.
          ls_poservices-service    = ls_bid_srv-servicenumber.
          ls_poservices-quantity   = ls_bid_srv-rfqqty.
          ls_poservices-base_uom   = ls_bid_srv-uom.
          ls_poservices-tax_code   = ls_bid_srv-taxcode.
          "BOC ali as per suggest by Vishwa
*          ls_poservices-gr_price   = ls_bid_srv-totamt.
          ls_poservices-gr_price   = ls_bid_srv-TOTUNITPRICE.
          "EOC ali as per suggest by Vishwa
          ls_poservices-pckg_no   = ls_poservices-subpckg_no.
          ls_poservices-subpckg_no = 000000000.
          append ls_poservices to lt_poservices.

          lv_serial_no = lv_serial_no + 1.
          """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Services Value data fill

          ls_poservicevalue-pckg_no    = ls_poservices-pckg_no.
          ls_poservicevalue-line_no    = ls_poservices-line_no.
          ls_poservicevalue-serno_line = lv_serial_no.
          ls_poservicevalue-serial_no = lv_serial_no.
          ls_poservicevalue-quantity = ls_poservices-quantity.
          ls_poservicevalue-net_value = ls_bid_srv-totamt.
          append ls_poservicevalue to lt_poservicevalue.
          clear ls_poservicevalue.

          """"""""""""""""""""""""""""" Services Value data fill

        endif.
      endloop.



*              endloop.
      "----------------------------
      " 5) CALL BAPI
      "----------------------------
      at end of awardedsupplier.
        if ls_poheader is not initial and lt_poitems is not initial.

          call function 'BAPI_PO_CREATE1'
            exporting
              poheader          = ls_poheader
              poheaderx         = ls_poheaderx
            importing
              exppurchaseorder  = lv_ponumber
            tables
              return            = lt_return
              poitem            = lt_poitems
              poitemx           = lt_poitemsx
              poschedule        = lt_poschedules
              poschedulex       = lt_poschedulesx
              poaccount         = lt_poaccount
              poaccountx        = lt_poaccountx
              pocond            = lt_POCOND
              pocondx           = lt_POCONDX
              poservices        = lt_poservices
              posrvaccessvalues = lt_poservicevalue
              extensionin       = lt_extensionin.

          "----------------------------
          " 6) HANDLE MESSAGES
          "----------------------------
          loop at lt_return assigning field-symbol(<ls_ret>) where ( type = 'E' or type = 'A' ) and id <> 'BAPI' and id <> 'MEPO'.
            if lv_message is initial.
              lv_message = |{ <ls_ret>-message }|.
            else.
              lv_message = |{ lv_message }, { <ls_ret>-message }|.
            endif.
          endloop.

          "----------------------------
          " 7) COMMIT or ROLLBACK
          "----------------------------
          if lv_message = 'Please maintain UID( LD Clause ) .'.
            clear lv_message.
          endif.
          if lv_message is initial.
            call function 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
              exporting
                wait = 'X'.
            lv_message = |PO created.|.
          else.
            call function 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK'.
          endif.
        else.
          lv_message = |PO not created.|.
        endif.

        clear : ls_poitems, ls_poitemsx, ls_poschedules, ls_poschedulesX , lt_poitems, lt_poitemsx, lt_poschedules, lt_poschedulesX,ls_eban.
      endat.

      ls_header-awardedsupplier = |{ ls_header-awardedsupplier alpha = in }|.
      select single name1 from lfa1 where lifnr = @ls_header-awardedsupplier into @lv_name1.
      ls_header-awardedsupplier = |{ ls_header-awardedsupplier alpha = out }|.
      append value #(
        nfaid        = |{ nfaid alpha = out }|
        prnumber     = ls_header-prnumber
        pritem       = ls_header-pritem
        material     = ls_header-matnr
        materialdesc = ls_header-maktx
        vendor       = ls_header-awardedsupplier
        vendorname   = lv_name1
        nfaamount    = ls_header-nfaamt
        doctype      = ls_poheader-doc_type
        companycode  = ls_header-bukrs
        ponumber     = lv_ponumber
        Message      = lv_message ) to return.

      clear : ls_header, lv_EKORG, ls_purgroup,
              ls_poheader,ls_poheaderx,
              lv_ponumber,lt_return , lv_name1.


    endloop.

*    *            ls_poheader-incoterms1 = ls_header4-del_term.
*            select single bezei from tinct into @data(lv_BEZEI) where inco1 = @ls_header4-del_term and spras = 'E'.
*            if lv_BEZEI is not initial.
*              ls_poheader-incoterms2 = lv_BEZEI.
*              endif.

*              ls_poheaderx-incoterms1  = abap_true.
*              ls_poheaderx-incoterms2  = abap_true.

*              loop at lt_header into data(ls_item) where prnumber = ls_header-prnumber and pritem = ls_header-pritem.
  endmethod.

**try.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->PRATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_PRATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PRATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
data : r_pritm  type RANGE OF  eban-purchasereqnitemuniqueid,
           l_pritm  like line of r_pritm,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset.

DATA: ls_draw             TYPE draw,
      lt_file             TYPE TABLE OF cvapi_doc_file,
          ls_file             TYPE cvapi_doc_file,
          lv_filename TYPE char200.
    try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'Key' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_pritm-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_pritm-sign   = 'I'.
            l_pritm-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_pritm to r_pritm.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.




Select drad~DOKAR,
       drad~DOKNR,
       drad~DOKVR,
       drad~DOKTL,
       drad~OBJKY ,
       drad~DOKOB
  FROM drad
    INTO TABLE @DATA(IT_att) WHERE objky in @r_pritm.


 SELECT  a~DOKNR ,  a~pritmkey , a~folderid   , a~specification , a~SENDTOSUPPLIER , a~file_name
   FROM zrfq_attachment AS a
  WHERE pritmkey in @r_pritm
   INTO TABLE @DATA(it_attach).

SORT it_att by doknr.
delete ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_att COMPARING doknr.


loop at it_att INTO DATA(wa_att).


  CALL FUNCTION 'CVAPI_DOC_GETDETAIL'
      EXPORTING
        pf_dokar  = wa_att-dokar
        pf_doknr  = wa_att-doknr
        pf_dokvr  = wa_att-dokvr
        pf_doktl  = wa_att-doktl
      IMPORTING
        psx_draw  = ls_draw
      TABLES
        pt_files  = lt_file
      EXCEPTIONS
        not_found = 1
        OTHERS    = 2.


LOOP AT lt_file INTO LS_FILE.

er_entity-prnumber  = wa_att-objky+0(10).
er_entity-pritem  = wa_att-objky+10(5).



SPLIT ls_file-filename at  '\' INTO TABLE DATA(it_parts).

READ TABLE it_parts INTO lv_filename INDEX lines( it_parts ).

er_entity-link = lv_filename.
er_entity-key = wa_att-objky.
ER_ENTITY-FOLDERID = LS_FILE-ph_objid.
READ TABLE it_attach INTO DATA(wa_attch) with key pritmkey = wa_att-objky
folderid = LS_FILE-ph_objid  file_name = lv_filename.
IF sy-subrc = 0.
er_entity-specification = wa_attch-specification.
er_entity-sendven = wa_attch-sendtosupplier.
ENDIF.
*er_entity-mimetype = wa_att-mimetype
*er_entity-objecttype = wa_att-obj_type.
er_entity-detail = 'PR Attachment'.
 append er_entity to et_entityset.
 CLEAR : ER_ENTITY , LS_FILE .

ENDLOOP.
CLEAR : wa_att.

  ENDLOOP.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_QUERYSET
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITYSET.

data : r_nfaid    type range of znfa_header-nfa_id,
           l_nfaid    like line of r_nfaid.

    try.
        DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
        DATA(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        l_NFAID-low = |{ ls_selopt-low ALPHA = IN }|.
        l_NFAID-sign   = 'I'.
        l_NFAID-option = 'EQ'.
        append  l_NFAID to r_NFAID.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.


    select nfaid,
           eventid ,
           CONCAT( question, question1 ) as question,
           CONCAT( answer, answer1 ) as answer,
           querychk,
           raisedby,
           raisedon
       from zgnfa_query_tab
      where nfaid in @r_nfaid
      into corresponding fields of table @et_entityset.


*LOOP AT ET_ENTITYSET ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<FS>).
*  IF <FS>-answer IS NOT INITIAL.
*    <FS>-querychk = ' '.
*
*    ENDIF.
*
*    ENDLOOP.



  endmethod.

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH

  method RECALLNFASET_CREATE_ENTITY.

DATA : LS_DATA TYPE ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>ts_recallnfa.

data(NFAID) = VALUE #( IT_KEY_TAB[ NAME = 'NFAID' ]-value OPTIONAL ).

IO_DATA_PROVIDER->read_entry_data(
  importing
    es_data = LS_DATA
).
*catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception. " mgw technical exception

UPDATE ZGNFA_HISTORY SET ACTION = 'RC' ,
                         edate = @SY-DATLO,
                         etime  = @SY-uzeit,
                         remark = @LS_DATA-remark
WHERE NFAID = @LS_DATA-NFAID.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
UPDATE ZNFA_HEADER SET draft = 'X',
                        status = 'D',
                        alevel = ' '
                        WHERE nfa_id = @LS_DATA-NFAID.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->RFQATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_RFQATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method RFQATTACHMENTSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
data : r_rfqid  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-rfqid,
           l_rfqid  like line of r_rfqid,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset.

    try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'RFQID' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_rfqid-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_rfqid-sign   = 'I'.
            l_rfqid-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_rfqid to r_rfqid.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.


SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT

  FIELDS
  RFQID,
  FILE_NAME,
  UPLOADED_BY,
  DOKNR,
  Specification,
  file_type,
  SENDTOSUPPLIER
  WHERE RFQID IN @r_rfqid and attachmenttype = 'RFQ'
  INTO  TABLE @DATA(IT_DATA).

et_entityset =  VALUE #( for LS_DATA IN IT_DATA INDEX INTO IND (

                         SRNO = IND
                         Specification = ls_data-Specification
                         FileName = ls_data-FILE_NAME
                         FileType = ls_data-file_type
                         DocID  = ls_data-DOKNR
                         SendToSupplier = ls_data-SENDTOSUPPLIER
                         RFQID = ls_data-rfqid

                         ) ).

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->SEND_MAIL
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] I_APPROVER                     TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_REASON                       TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_VENDOR                       TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_BUKRS                        TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_PO                           TYPE        CHAR10(optional)
* | [--->] I_RECIPIENT                    TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_BUYER                        TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_NFAID                        TYPE        CHAR10(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILTO                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILCC                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_SUBJECT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_CONTENT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [<---] RETURN                         TYPE        CHAR10
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method send_mail.
    types: begin of ty_email,
             email type ad_smtpadr,
           end of ty_email.

    data: lt_email type table of ty_email,
          ls_email type ty_email.

    data: lt_mailrecipients type standard table of somlrec90,
          ls_mailrecipients type somlrec90,
          ls_mailsubject    type sodocchgi1,
          lt_packing_list   type standard table of sopcklsti1,
          ls_packing_list   type sopcklsti1,
          gv_cnt            type i,
          lv_partner        type bu_partner,
          lv_vendor         type lifnr,
          wa_spfli          type spfli.

    data: addsmtp   type table of bapiadsmtp,
          it_return type table of bapiret2.

    data:lv_month(2) type c,
         lv_year(4)  type c.
    data:lv_date type char10.
    data:lv_date1 type datum.

    data: lo_send_request  type ref to  cl_bcs,
          lo_document      type ref to  cl_document_bcs,
          lo_recipient     type ref to  if_recipient_bcs,
          lo_bcs_exception type ref to  cx_bcs,
          lt_mailtxt       type standard table of solisti1,
          l_mailtxt        type solisti1,
          sender1          type ref to cl_cam_address_bcs,
          lv_sent_to_all   type os_boolean,
          lv_subject       type char50,
          lv_nfaid         type char50.

    data: lo_sender        type ref to if_sender_bcs value is initial. " Added by shahrukh 08/01/2026.
    if i_nfaid is not initial.
      lv_nfaid = |{ i_nfaid alpha = out }|.
    endif.
    case i_content.
      when 'MAIL1'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_approver },</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |NFA { lv_nfaid } is pending for your approval.</br>| )
          ( line = |Please review and approve at the earliest.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |<a href="https://azureportalprod-cqtz7hbe.launchpad.cfapps.in30.hana.ondemand.com/site?siteId=fb5c783b-a296-47c9-b4d3-a1a5d4285ace#Shell-home">Click here to view</a></br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
          ( line = |Procurement Team| )
          ( line = |</BODY></HTML>| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL2'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Approver details missing against the NFA { lv_nfaid } initiated for Approval.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Kindly click here to vire the NFA details and maintain the appropriate approver details and resubmit for approval.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Thanks,| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |This is system generated E Mail.| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL3'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
    ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
    ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
    ( line = |<br/>| )
    ( line = |NFA { lv_nfaid } has been approved at all levels.</br>| )
    ( line = |Please proceed with PO creation.</br>| )
    ( line = |<br/>| )
    ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
    ( line = |Procurement Team| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL4'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_recipient },</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |PO { i_po } has been successfully created.</br>| )
          ( line = |Vendor: { i_vendor }</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Please review and proceed with further actions.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
          ( line = |Procurement Team| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL5'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |NFA { lv_nfaid } has been rejected by { i_approver } due to the following reason:</br>| )
          ( line = |{ i_reason }.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
          ( line = |Procurement Team| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL6'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |A Query has been raised by { i_approver }.</br>| )
          ( line = |Please provide the required response on time to process it further.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
          ( line = |Procurement Team| )
        ).
      when 'MAIL7'.
        lt_mailtxt = value #(
          ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
          ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear Vendor On Boarding Team,</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |There is an NFA created for the Vendor { i_vendor } for Company Code { i_bukrs }.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Kindly extend the Vendor Code against the Company Code for proceeding with NFA.</br>| )
          ( line = |<br/>| )
          ( line = |Regards,<br/>| )
          ( line = |Procurement Team| )
        ).
    endcase.

    try.
*     -------- create persistent send request ------------------------
        lo_send_request = cl_bcs=>create_persistent( ).

*     -------- create and set document -------------------------------
        lv_subject = i_subject.
        lo_document = cl_document_bcs=>create_document(
          i_type    = 'HTM'
          i_text    = lt_mailtxt
          i_subject = lv_subject ).


        data:lv_send type string.
        lv_send  = sy-uname.
        call method lo_send_request->set_document( lo_document ).

        try.
            lo_sender = cl_sapuser_bcs=>create( sy-uname ).
            lo_send_request->set_sender(
              exporting
                i_sender = lo_sender ).
          catch cx_address_bcs.
****Catch exception here
        endtry.
        """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""EOC.


        loop at i_emailto into ls_email.
          if ls_email-email is not initial.
            lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
              ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
            call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
              exporting
                i_recipient = lo_recipient
                i_express   = 'X'.
            clear ls_email.
          endif.
        endloop.
        loop at i_emailcc into ls_email.
          if ls_email-email is not initial.
            lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
              ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
            call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
              exporting
                i_recipient = lo_recipient
                i_copy      = 'X'.
            clear ls_email.
          endif.
        endloop.

        call method lo_send_request->set_send_immediately
          exporting
            i_send_immediately = 'X'.

        call method lo_send_request->send(
          exporting
            i_with_error_screen = 'X'
          receiving
            result              = lv_sent_to_all ).
        if lv_sent_to_all is initial.
          return = 'X'.
        else.
          commit work and wait.
        endif.
      catch cx_bcs into lo_bcs_exception.
    endtry.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TS_VENDORHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITY.
**try.
*CALL METHOD SUPER->VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITY
*  EXPORTING
*    IV_ENTITY_NAME          =
*    IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME      =
*    IV_SOURCE_NAME          =
*    IT_KEY_TAB              =
**    io_request_object       =
**    io_tech_request_context =
*    IT_NAVIGATION_PATH      =
**  importing
**    er_entity               =
**    es_response_context     =
*    .
**  catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception.
**  catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception.
**endtry.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_DPC_EXT->VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>TT_VENDORHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET.

 DATA: lv_top   TYPE string,
        lv_skip  TYPE i,
      r_EVENT    type range of zrfq_header-event_no,
           l_EVENT    like line of r_EVENT.
**- Get number of records requested
  CLEAR: lv_top,lv_skip.
  lv_top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).

**- Get number of lines that should be skipped
  lv_skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).
  lv_top = lv_top + lv_skip.

    try.
        DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'EventID' ].
        DATA(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        l_EVENT-low = |{ ls_selopt-low ALPHA = IN }|.
        l_EVENT-sign   = 'I'.
        l_EVENT-option = 'EQ'.
        append  l_EVENT to r_eVENT.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

SELECT DISTINCT SUPPLIER as vendor , SUPPLIERNAME as name , supplierref as SupplierRefrence
  FROM ZRFQ_item_BID_RE
  WHERE event_no IN @r_event
   INTO  CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @et_entityset.




**- Skip/Top Logic to Restrict No Of Records
  IF lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_skip <> '0'.
    DELETE et_entityset FROM 1 TO lv_skip.
  ENDIF.
  endmethod.
ENDCLASS.
